# Analyzed PTCy vs CNI+MTX/MMF in allogeneic HSCT via Bayesian meta-analysis across six primary outcomes. Completed all M1/M2/M3 models, IFI leave-one-out sensitivity (Yanada 2026 drives heterogeneity without affecting OR), built a six-outcome summary forest plot, drafted the infection outcomes Results section (~1,050 words), and drafted the mechanistic asymmetry Discussion paragraph (~590 words) framing two orthogonal PTCy pathways: GVHD suppression (NRM complete mediation, OS partial mediation) versus T-cell lymphodepletion (CMV/BSI steroid-independent elevation), with IFI reframed as a prophylaxis confounding story.

## User

Review the folder contents and the STATUS.md document and lets begin the Bayesian meta-analysis

Also runs lightweight validation checks.\n#\n# Usage:\n#   Rscript export_to_csv.R\n# or from within R:\n#   source(\"export_to_csv.R\")\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nsuppressPackageStartupMessages({\n  library(readxl)\n  library(dplyr)\n  library(readr)\n  library(tibble)\n  library(stringr)\n  library(purrr)\n})\n\nXLSX <- \"PTCy_MA_extraction_template_v1.0.xlsx\"\nOUT_DIR <- file.path(\"02_extraction\")\nif (!dir.exists(OUT_DIR)) dir.create(OUT_DIR, recursive = TRUE)\n\n# Sheets to export. README and codebook are reference only.\nDATA_SHEETS <- c(\"cohorts\", \"studies\", \"arms\", \"outcomes\",\n                 \"outcomes_subgroup\", \"rob\")\n\n# The first 3 rows of each data sheet are: header, required/optional flag,\n# field notes. Data starts at row 4.\nread_sheet <- function(sheet) {\n  hdr <- read_excel(XLSX, sheet = sheet, n_max = 1, col_names = FALSE,\n                    .name_repair = \"minimal\")\n  col_names <- as.character(unlist(hdr[1, ]))\n  df <- read_excel(XLSX, sheet = sheet, skip = 3, col_names = col_names,\n                   .name_repair = \"minimal\")\n  # Drop rows that are completely empty\n  keep <- rowSums(!is.na(df) & df != \"\") > 0\n  df[keep, , drop = FALSE]\n}\n\n# ---- read everything ------------------------------------------------------\nmessage(\"Reading workbook ...\")\ntabs <- map(set_names(DATA_SHEETS, DATA_SHEETS), read_sheet)\nwalk2(DATA_SHEETS, tabs, ~ message(sprintf(\"  %s: %d rows × %d cols\",\n                                            .x, nrow(.y), ncol(.y))))\n\n# ---- type coercion --------------------------------------------------------\n# Excel will return everything as character if any cell holds NR/UNCL etc.\n# That's fine for export; downstream brms code coerces numerics with\n# vapply(x, function(v) ifelse(v %in% c(\"NR\",\"NA\",\"UNCL\",\"PEND\"), NA_real_,\n#                              as.numeric(v)), 0)\n\n# ---- write CSVs -----------------------------------------------------------\nmessage(\"\\nWriting CSVs to \", OUT_DIR, \" ...\")\niwalk(tabs, function(df, name) {\n  path <- file.path(OUT_DIR, paste0(name, \".csv\"))\n  write_csv(df, path, na = \"\")\n  message(\"  wrote \", path)\n})\n\n# ---- validation checks ----------------------------------------------------\nmessage(\"\\nValidation checks:\")\n\nproblems <- list()\n\n# 1. event_count <= denominator_n in outcomes / outcomes_subgroup\ncheck_events <- function(df, label) {\n  if (!all(c(\"event_count\", \"denominator_n\") %in% names(df))) return()\n  bad <- df |>\n    mutate(\n      events_num = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(event_count)),\n      denom_num  = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(denominator_n))\n    ) |>\n    filter(!is.na(events_num), !is.na(denom_num), events_num > denom_num)\n  if (nrow(bad) > 0) {\n    problems[[paste0(label, \":events>n\")]] <<- bad\n    message(sprintf(\"  [FAIL] %s: %d rows have event_count > denominator_n\",\n                    label, nrow(bad)))\n  } else {\n    message(sprintf(\"  [OK]   %s: events <= n in all rows\", label))\n  }\n}\ncheck_events(tabs$outcomes, \"outcomes\")\ncheck_events(tabs$outcomes_subgroup, \"outcomes_subgroup\")\n\n# 2. denominator_n matches arms.n_patients (allowing attrition flagged in notes)\nif (nrow(tabs$outcomes) > 0 && nrow(tabs$arms) > 0) {\n  joined <- tabs$outcomes |>\n    mutate(arm_id = suppressWarnings(as.integer(arm_id)),\n           denom_num = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(denominator_n))) |>\n    left_join(tabs$arms |>\n                mutate(arm_id = suppressWarnings(as.integer(arm_id)),\n                       n_arm = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(n_patients))) |>\n                select(arm_id, n_arm), by = \"arm_id\") |>\n    filter(!is.na(denom_num), !is.na(n_arm), denom_num > n_arm)\n  if (nrow(joined) > 0) {\n    problems[[\"outcomes:denom>n_arm\"]] <- joined\n    message(sprintf(\"  [WARN] outcomes: %d rows have denominator_n > arms.n_patients (attrition?)\",\n                    nrow(joined)))\n  } else {\n    message(\"  [OK]   outcomes: denominator_n <= arms.n_patients in all rows\")\n  }\n}\n\n# 3. % columns each sum to <=100 per arm\npct_groups <- list(\n  disease = c(\"disease_aml_pct\",\"disease_all_pct\",\"disease_mds_pct\",\n              \"disease_mpn_pct\",\"disease_cml_pct\",\"disease_lymphoma_pct\",\n              \"disease_mm_pct\",\"disease_cll_pct\",\"disease_saa_pct\",\n              \"disease_other_pct\"),\n  donor   = c(\"donor_msd_pct\",\"donor_mud_pct\",\"donor_mmud_pct\",\n              \"donor_haplo_pct\",\"donor_ucb_pct\"),\n  graft   = c(\"graft_pbsc_pct\",\"graft_bm_pct\",\"graft_ucb_pct\"),\n  conditioning = c(\"conditioning_mac_pct\",\"conditioning_ric_pct\",\n                   \"conditioning_nma_pct\")\n)\nif (nrow(tabs$arms) > 0) {\n  for (grp in names(pct_groups)) {\n    cols <- intersect(pct_groups[[grp]], names(tabs$arms))\n    if (length(cols) < 2) next\n    df_num <- tabs$arms |>\n      select(arm_id, all_of(cols)) |>\n      mutate(across(all_of(cols),\n                    ~ suppressWarnings(as.numeric(.x))))\n    df_num$pct_sum <- rowSums(df_num[, cols], na.rm = TRUE)\n    bad <- df_num |> filter(pct_sum > 100.5)\n    if (nrow(bad) > 0) {\n      problems[[paste0(\"arms:\", grp, \"_pct_sum>100\")]] <- bad\n      message(sprintf(\"  [WARN] arms: %d rows have %s percentages summing to >100\",\n                      nrow(bad), grp))\n    } else {\n      message(sprintf(\"  [OK]   arms: %s percentages <=100 in all rows\", grp))\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n# 4. comparison eligibility — each eligible arm has a comparator in same study\nif (nrow(tabs$arms) > 0) {\n  for (cmp in c(\"comparison_1_eligible\", \"comparison_2_eligible\",\n                \"comparison_3_eligible\")) {\n    if (!cmp %in% names(tabs$arms)) next\n    elig <- tabs$arms |>\n      filter(.data[[cmp]] == \"Y\") |>\n      group_by(study_id) |>\n      summarise(n_elig = n(), .groups = \"drop\") |>\n      filter(n_elig < 2)\n    if (nrow(elig) > 0) {\n      message(sprintf(\"  [WARN] %s: %d study/ies have <2 eligible arms\",\n                      cmp, nrow(elig)))\n    } else {\n      message(sprintf(\"  [OK]   %s: every eligible arm has a comparator\", cmp))\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n# ---- write validation report ---------------------------------------------\nif (length(problems) > 0) {\n  rpt <- file.path(OUT_DIR, \"_validation_failures.rds\")\n  saveRDS(problems, rpt)\n  message(\"\\nValidation failures saved to \", rpt)\n  message(\"Inspect with: readRDS('\", rpt, \"')\")\n} else {\n  message(\"\\nAll validation checks passed.\")\n}\n\n# ---- analytic-dataset builder (convenience) ------------------------------\nbuild_analytic <- function(category, subtype, timepoint,\n                            comparison = 1, only_primary = TRUE) {\n  cmp_col <- paste0(\"comparison_\", comparison, \"_eligible\")\n  tabs$outcomes |>\n    mutate(arm_id    = suppressWarnings(as.integer(arm_id)),\n           study_id  = suppressWarnings(as.integer(study_id)),\n           cohort_id = suppressWarnings(as.integer(cohort_id)),\n           event_count    = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(event_count)),\n           denominator_n  = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(denominator_n))) |>\n    filter(outcome_category == category,\n           outcome_subtype  == subtype,\n           timepoint == !!timepoint,\n           if (only_primary) is_primary_definition == \"Y\" else TRUE) |>\n    inner_join(\n      tabs$arms |> mutate(arm_id = suppressWarnings(as.integer(arm_id))),\n      by = \"arm_id\"\n    ) |>\n    inner_join(\n      tabs$studies |>\n        mutate(study_id = suppressWarnings(as.integer(study_id))) |>\n        select(study_id, cohort_id, pub_year, region, era_category,\n               primary_for_cohort),\n      by = c(\"study_id\", \"cohort_id\")\n    ) |>\n    filter(.data[[cmp_col]] == \"Y\",\n           primary_for_cohort %in% c(\"Y\", \"partial\"))\n}\n\nmessage(\"\\nDone. \",\n        \"Call build_analytic(category, subtype, timepoint, comparison=N) \",\n        \"to build a brms-ready dataset.\")\n"},"elapsedMs":5}</script></tool-result></div>

### Study design breakdown
- Retrospective cohort: 119 · Registry: 59 · **RCT: 10** · Prospective: 7 · Single-arm (excluded): 25

### Comparison 1 arm split (301 arms)
- **PTCy = Y:** 142 · **Control = N:** 151 · Mixed: 8 — well-balanced for meta-analysis

### Validation warnings to be aware of

| Warning | Count | Assessment |
|---|---|---|
| `denominator_n > n_patients` | 30 rows | Mostly study 226 (Luznik/BMT_CTN_1301) with OS denominators > arm size — likely competing-risk/KM denominators. Plus the known Alousi 2015 flag (outcome 225). **Low risk for binary outcomes; will need review before survival modeling.** |
| Disease % sum > 100 | 10 arms | Small rounding errors (e.g. 100.1%). Arms 12, 13, 121, 233, 261, 265, 266, 278, 341, 366 |
| Donor % sum > 100 | 6 arms | Same — rounding. Arms 62, 278, 359, 366, 377, 386 |
| Graft % sum > 100 | 4 arms | Arms 105, 240, 341, 415 |
| Conditioning % sum > 100 | 2 arms | Arms 99, 248 |

The % rounding issues are cosmetic and won't affect the analysis. The denominator mismatch cluster in study 226 is worth noting for the survival sub-models.

### All comparison eligibility checks: ✅ PASS

Good — now I have a clear picture. Only a subset of RCTs have usable (non-NR) CMV data. Let me build the metafor dataset and run the model:

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01QVWsyiXvADyGEzNehAhXJW"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"library(metafor)\nlibrary(dplyr)\nlibrary(tidyr)\n\n# ── Step 1: build RCT-only CMV analytic d...[truncated, total 3558 chars]","intent":"Build RCT-only CMV analytic dataset and pivot to wide format for metafor","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Build RCT-only CMV analytic dataset and pivot to wide format for metafor","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"library(metafor)\nlibrary(dplyr)\nlibrary(tidyr)\n\n# ── Step 1: build RCT-only CMV analytic dataset ──────────────────────────────\n# Best-powered CMV endpoint: any_reactivation (most arms)\n# Best timepoints in RCTs: D+100, D+180, end_of_followup\n# Strategy: for each RCT study, take the most informative non-NR CMV row.\n# Priority: D+100 > D+180 > end_of_followup, subtype any_reactivation first,\n#           then csCMV.\n\nrct_ids <- studies |> filter(study_design == \"RCT\") |> pull(study_id)\n\n# Pull all Comparison-1-eligible CMV rows for RCTs with numeric data\ncmv_rct_raw <- outcomes |>\n  filter(outcome_category == \"CMV\",\n         study_id %in% rct_ids) |>\n  inner_join(\n    arms |> select(arm_id, study_id, comparison_1_eligible, ptcy_used,\n                   n_patients, arm_role),\n    by = c(\"arm_id\", \"study_id\")\n  ) |>\n  filter(comparison_1_eligible == \"Y\") |>\n  mutate(\n    events_n = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(event_count)),\n    denom_n  = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(denominator_n))\n  ) |>\n  filter(!is.na(events_n), !is.na(denom_n))   # drop NR rows\n\ncat(\"=== Usable (non-NR) CMV rows in RCTs, Comparison 1 ===\\n\")\ncmv_rct_raw |>\n  select(study_id, arm_id, ptcy_used, outcome_subtype, timepoint,\n         events_n, denom_n) |>\n  arrange(study_id, arm_id) |>\n  print()\n\n# ── Step 2: pick best row per arm (timepoint priority) ───────────────────────\ntp_rank <- c(D100 = 1, D180 = 2, eof = 3, D365 = 4)\n\ncmv_rct <- cmv_rct_raw |>\n  mutate(tp_priority = case_when(\n    timepoint == \"D+100\"           ~ 1L,\n    timepoint == \"D+180\"           ~ 2L,\n    timepoint == \"end_of_followup\" ~ 3L,\n    timepoint == \"D+365_1yr\"       ~ 4L,\n    TRUE                           ~ 5L\n  ),\n  st_priority = case_when(\n    outcome_subtype == \"any_reactivation\"          ~ 1L,\n    outcome_subtype == \"clinically_significant_csCMV\" ~ 2L,\n    outcome_subtype == \"CMV_end_organ_disease\"     ~ 3L,\n    TRUE                                            ~ 4L\n  )) |>\n  group_by(arm_id) |>\n  slice_min(order_by = tp_priority * 10 + st_priority, n = 1, with_ties = FALSE) |>\n  ungroup()\n\ncat(\"\\n=== Selected CMV row per arm ===\\n\")\ncmv_rct |>\n  select(study_id, arm_id, ptcy_used, outcome_subtype, timepoint,\n         events_n, denom_n) |>\n  arrange(study_id) |>\n  print()\n\n# ── Step 3: pivot to wide (PTCy arm vs Control arm) per study ────────────────\ncmv_wide <- cmv_rct |>\n  select(study_id, arm_id, ptcy_used, outcome_subtype, timepoint,\n         events_n, denom_n) |>\n  pivot_wider(\n    id_cols    = study_id,\n    names_from = ptcy_used,\n    values_from = c(events_n, denom_n, outcome_subtype, timepoint, arm_id),\n    values_fn  = list(events_n = first, denom_n = first,\n                      outcome_subtype = first, timepoint = first,\n                      arm_id = first)\n  ) |>\n  # Rename to metafor-standard: ai/bi = ptcy events/non-events; ci/di = ctrl\n  rename_with(~ gsub(\"events_n_Y$\", \"ai\", .x)) |>\n  rename_with(~ gsub(\"events_n_N$\", \"ci\", .x)) |>\n  rename_with(~ gsub(\"denom_n_Y$\",  \"n1i\", .x)) |>\n  rename_with(~ gsub(\"denom_n_N$\",  \"n2i\", .x)) |>\n  mutate(\n    bi = n1i - ai,\n    di = n2i - ci,\n    study_label = case_when(\n      study_id == 79  ~ \"Brissot 2024 (EBMT RCT)\",\n      study_id == 80  ~ \"Broers 2022 (HOVON-96)\",\n      study_id == 105 ~ \"Curtis 2025 (ALLG BM12-CAST)\",\n      study_id == 397 ~ \"Xu 2025 (ATG+PTCy vs ATG)\",\n      TRUE            ~ paste0(\"Study \", study_id)\n    )\n  )\n\ncat(\"\\n=== Wide CMV dataset for metafor ===\\n\")\ncmv_wide |>\n  select(study_id, study_label, ai, n1i, ci, n2i,\n         outcome_subtype_Y, timepoint_Y) |>\n  print()","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

For an\nintroduction to the package please type: help(metafor)\n\nWarning messages:\n1: package ‘metafor’ was built under R version 4.5.2 \n2: package ‘Matrix’ was built under R version 4.5.2 \n3: package ‘metadat’ was built under R version 4.5.2 \n\nAttaching package: ‘tidyr’\n\nThe following objects are masked from ‘package:Matrix’:\n\n    expand, pack, unpack\n\nWarning message:\npackage ‘tidyr’ was built under R version 4.5.2 \n=== Usable (non-NR) CMV rows in RCTs, Comparison 1 ===\n# A tibble: 2 × 7\n  study_id arm_id ptcy_used outcome_subtype  timepoint events_n\n     <dbl>  <dbl> <chr>     <chr>            <chr>        <dbl>\n1      105    120 Y         clinically_sign… D+100           14\n2      105    121 N         clinically_sign… D+100           10\n# ℹ 1 more variable: denom_n <dbl>\n\n=== Selected CMV row per arm ===\n# A tibble: 2 × 7\n  study_id arm_id ptcy_used outcome_subtype  timepoint events_n\n     <dbl>  <dbl> <chr>     <chr>            <chr>        <dbl>\n1      105    120 Y         clinically_sign… D+100           14\n2      105    121 N         clinically_sign… D+100           10\n# ℹ 1 more variable: denom_n <dbl>\n\n=== Wide CMV dataset for metafor ===\n# A tibble: 1 × 8\n  study_id study_label                     ai   n1i    ci   n2i\n     <dbl> <chr>                        <dbl> <dbl> <dbl> <dbl>\n1      105 Curtis 2025 (ALLG BM12-CAST)    14    61    10    68\n# ℹ 2 more variables: outcome_subtype_Y <chr>,\n#   timepoint_Y <chr>\n"},"elapsedMs":655}</script></tool-result></div>



Only 1 RCT has usable CMV data — the RCT subset is too sparse for a meta-analysis on its own. Let me check why the others dropped out, then pivot to the right approach:

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01SJXppB6UjkgKi14X1EHuEQ"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"cat(\"=== WHY OTHER RCTs HAVE NO USABLE CMV DATA ===\\n\")\n# Show all CMV rows for all RCTs, ...[truncated, total 1185 chars]","intent":"Understand which RCTs lack CMV data and why","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Understand which RCTs lack CMV data and why","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"cat(\"=== WHY OTHER RCTs HAVE NO USABLE CMV DATA ===\\n\")\n# Show all CMV rows for all RCTs, including NR\noutcomes |>\n  filter(outcome_category == \"CMV\", study_id %in% rct_ids) |>\n  inner_join(\n    arms |> select(arm_id, study_id, comparison_1_eligible, comparison_2_eligible,\n                   comparison_3_eligible, ptcy_used),\n    by = c(\"arm_id\", \"study_id\")\n  ) |>\n  mutate(events_n = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(event_count))) |>\n  select(study_id, arm_id, comparison_1_eligible, comparison_2_eligible,\n         comparison_3_eligible, ptcy_used, outcome_subtype, timepoint,\n         event_count, is_primary_definition) |>\n  arrange(study_id) |>\n  left_join(studies |> select(study_id, first_author, pub_year),\n            by = \"study_id\") |>\n  select(study_id, first_author, pub_year, arm_id,\n         comparison_1_eligible, ptcy_used,\n         outcome_subtype, timepoint, event_count) |>\n  print(n = 40)\n\ncat(\"\\n=== RCTs with NO CMV data at all ===\\n\")\nrct_ids[!rct_ids %in% (outcomes |> filter(outcome_category==\"CMV\") |> pull(study_id) |> unique())] |>\n  (\\(ids) studies |> filter(study_id %in% ids) |>\n     select(study_id, first_author, pub_year, study_design))() |>\n  print()","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01M7ZQyfYiKJJ7HhfAeQAFCZ"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"cat(\"=== EXPLANATION OF RCT CMV DATA GAPS ===\\n\")\n# Summary by RCT\nrct_summary <- tribble(...[truncated, total 2875 chars]","intent":"Understand RCT CMV gaps, then build full Comparison-1 CMV D+100 dataset for metafor","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Understand RCT CMV gaps, then build full Comparison-1 CMV D+100 dataset for metafor","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"cat(\"=== EXPLANATION OF RCT CMV DATA GAPS ===\\n\")\n# Summary by RCT\nrct_summary <- tribble(\n  ~study_id, ~first_author,     ~comp1, ~cmv_issue,\n  2,         \"Bolanos-Meade 2023 (BMT_CTN_1703)\", \"Y\", \"CMV extracted but event_count = NR\",\n  12,        \"Abedin 2025 (CTN_1703 ≥70yr)\",      \"Y\", \"No CMV row extracted\",\n  72,        \"Bolanos-Meade 2019 (CTN_1203)\",       \"Y\", \"No CMV row extracted\",\n  79,        \"Brissot 2024 (EBMT ATG RCT)\",         \"N\", \"Comparison 2 only — not Comp 1 eligible\",\n  80,        \"Broers 2022 (HOVON-96)\",               \"N\", \"Comparison 1 NOT eligible; CMV data available\",\n  105,       \"Curtis 2025 (ALLG BM12-CAST)\",         \"Y\", \"✅ csCMV D+100: ai=14/61 vs ci=10/68\",\n  140,       \"Fuchs 2021 (CTN_1101 Haplo/UCB)\",      \"~\", \"No CMV row (within-PTCy, Comp 3)\",\n  226,       \"Luznik 2022 (CTN_1301 CNI-free)\",      \"Y\", \"CMV extracted but event_count = NR\",\n  380,       \"Ueda Oshima 2025 (FHCC Siro+CsA+PTCy)\",\"Y\", \"CMV extracted but event_count = NR\",\n  397,       \"Xu 2025 (ATG+PTCy vs ATG)\",            \"N\", \"Comparison 2/3 only; CMV available but Comp 1 = N\"\n)\nprint(rct_summary, n = 10)\n\n# ── Now run metafor on ALL Comp-1 studies with usable CMV data ───────────────\ncat(\"\\n=== PIVOTING TO FULL CORPUS (all Comp-1 arms, not just RCTs) ===\\n\")\ncat(\"Building dataset: CMV any_reactivation | D+100 | Comparison 1\\n\\n\")\n\n# Use build_analytic from export script\ncmv_d100 <- build_analytic(\"CMV\", \"any_reactivation\", \"D+100\", comparison = 1) |>\n  mutate(\n    events_n = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(event_count)),\n    denom_n  = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(denominator_n))\n  ) |>\n  filter(!is.na(events_n), !is.na(denom_n))\n\ncat(sprintf(\"Arms with usable data: %d\\n\", nrow(cmv_d100)))\ncat(sprintf(\"Studies represented:   %d\\n\", n_distinct(cmv_d100$study_id)))\ncat(sprintf(\"PTCy arms: %d  |  Control arms: %d\\n\",\n            sum(cmv_d100$ptcy_used == \"Y\"), sum(cmv_d100$ptcy_used == \"N\")))\n\n# Pivot to wide per study\ncmv_d100_wide <- cmv_d100 |>\n  select(study_id, ptcy_used, events_n, denom_n) |>\n  pivot_wider(id_cols = study_id,\n              names_from = ptcy_used,\n              values_from = c(events_n, denom_n),\n              values_fn = first) |>\n  rename(ai = events_n_Y, ci = events_n_N,\n         n1i = denom_n_Y, n2i = denom_n_N) |>\n  filter(!is.na(ai), !is.na(ci), !is.na(n1i), !is.na(n2i)) |>\n  mutate(bi = n1i - ai, di = n2i - ci) |>\n  left_join(studies |> select(study_id, first_author, pub_year, study_design),\n            by = \"study_id\") |>\n  mutate(study_label = paste0(first_author, \" \", pub_year),\n         is_rct = study_design == \"RCT\")\n\ncat(sprintf(\"\\nPaired studies for metafor: %d\\n\", nrow(cmv_d100_wide)))\ncat(sprintf(\"RCTs among these: %d\\n\", sum(cmv_d100_wide$is_rct)))\ncmv_d100_wide |>\n  select(study_label, study_design, ai, n1i, ci, n2i) |>\n  arrange(desc(study_design == \"RCT\"), study_label) |>\n  print(n = 30)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01URWGnicED3mekW4F2TG4nw"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# build_analytic joins on both arm_id and study_id — fix by rebuilding manually\n# (the out...[truncated, total 2229 chars]","intent":"Manually build CMV any_reactivation D+100 Comparison-1 dataset and pivot wide for metafor","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Manually build CMV any_reactivation D+100 Comparison-1 dataset and pivot wide for metafor","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# build_analytic joins on both arm_id and study_id — fix by rebuilding manually\n# (the outcomes sheet's study_id comes from the arm join not the outcome row)\n\ncat(\"=== BUILD CMV ANY_REACTIVATION D+100, COMPARISON 1 ===\\n\")\n\ncmv_d100 <- outcomes |>\n  mutate(\n    arm_id    = suppressWarnings(as.integer(arm_id)),\n    events_n  = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(event_count)),\n    denom_n   = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(denominator_n))\n  ) |>\n  filter(\n    outcome_category    == \"CMV\",\n    outcome_subtype     == \"any_reactivation\",\n    timepoint           == \"D+100\",\n    is_primary_definition == \"Y\",\n    !is.na(events_n), !is.na(denom_n)\n  ) |>\n  inner_join(\n    arms |>\n      mutate(arm_id = suppressWarnings(as.integer(arm_id))) |>\n      select(arm_id, study_id, comparison_1_eligible, ptcy_used, n_patients),\n    by = \"arm_id\"\n  ) |>\n  filter(comparison_1_eligible == \"Y\") |>\n  inner_join(\n    studies |>\n      mutate(study_id = suppressWarnings(as.integer(study_id))) |>\n      select(study_id, cohort_id, first_author, pub_year, study_design,\n             primary_for_cohort),\n    by = \"study_id\"\n  ) |>\n  filter(primary_for_cohort %in% c(\"Y\", \"partial\"))\n\ncat(sprintf(\"Arms with usable data: %d\\n\", nrow(cmv_d100)))\ncat(sprintf(\"PTCy arms: %d  |  Control: %d\\n\",\n            sum(cmv_d100$ptcy_used == \"Y\"), sum(cmv_d100$ptcy_used == \"N\")))\n\n# Pivot wide\ncmv_wide <- cmv_d100 |>\n  select(study_id, cohort_id, ptcy_used, events_n, denom_n,\n         first_author, pub_year, study_design) |>\n  pivot_wider(\n    id_cols    = c(study_id, cohort_id, first_author, pub_year, study_design),\n    names_from = ptcy_used,\n    values_from = c(events_n, denom_n),\n    values_fn  = first\n  ) |>\n  rename(ai = events_n_Y, ci = events_n_N,\n         n1i = denom_n_Y, n2i = denom_n_N) |>\n  filter(!is.na(ai), !is.na(ci), !is.na(n1i), !is.na(n2i)) |>\n  mutate(bi = n1i - ai, di = n2i - ci,\n         study_label = paste0(first_author, \" \", pub_year),\n         is_rct = study_design == \"RCT\")\n\ncat(sprintf(\"\\nPaired studies for metafor: %d\\n\", nrow(cmv_wide)))\ncat(sprintf(\"RCTs among these: %d\\n\", sum(cmv_wide$is_rct)))\n\ncmv_wide |>\n  arrange(desc(is_rct), study_label) |>\n  select(study_label, study_design, ai, n1i, ci, n2i) |>\n  print(n = 40)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01KtgDera974DKebWifjaqG6"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# The issue is likely that outcomes$study_id is character, arms$study_id is numeric\n# Let'...[truncated, total 296 chars]","intent":"Check column types causing join failure","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Check column types causing join failure","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# The issue is likely that outcomes$study_id is character, arms$study_id is numeric\n# Let's be explicit with types\ncat(\"outcomes$study_id class:\", class(outcomes$study_id), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"arms$study_id class:\", class(arms$study_id), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"studies$study_id class:\", class(studies$study_id), \"\\n\")","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01KtgDera974DKebWifjaqG6"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"content","value":[{"type":"text","text":"outcomes$study_id class: numeric \narms$study_id class: numeric \nstudies$study_id class: numeric \n"}]},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"outcomes$study_id class: numeric \narms$study_id class: numeric \nstudies$study_id class: numeric \n"},"elapsedMs":5}</script></tool-result></div>



<div><tool-call id="toolu_01Qu8SmM9xN9udgK5WrKz5gC"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# The error must be coming from the arms join overwriting study_id\n# Let's step through ca...[truncated, total 2083 chars]","intent":"Step-by-step build of CMV D+100 wide dataset, fixing the join issue","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Step-by-step build of CMV D+100 wide dataset, fixing the join issue","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# The error must be coming from the arms join overwriting study_id\n# Let's step through carefully\n\nstep1 <- outcomes |>\n  filter(\n    outcome_category      == \"CMV\",\n    outcome_subtype       == \"any_reactivation\",\n    timepoint             == \"D+100\",\n    is_primary_definition == \"Y\"\n  ) |>\n  mutate(\n    events_n = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(event_count)),\n    denom_n  = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(denominator_n))\n  ) |>\n  filter(!is.na(events_n), !is.na(denom_n))\n\ncat(\"step1 rows:\", nrow(step1), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"step1 cols with study:\", names(step1)[grepl(\"study\", names(step1))], \"\\n\")\n\n# Join arms — note arms also has study_id; use suffix to keep both then reconcile\nstep2 <- step1 |>\n  left_join(\n    arms |> select(arm_id, study_id, comparison_1_eligible, ptcy_used, n_patients),\n    by = \"arm_id\",\n    suffix = c(\"\", \"_arm\")\n  ) |>\n  filter(comparison_1_eligible == \"Y\")\n\ncat(\"step2 rows:\", nrow(step2), \"\\n\")\n\n# Join studies\nstep3 <- step2 |>\n  left_join(\n    studies |> select(study_id, cohort_id, first_author, pub_year,\n                      study_design, primary_for_cohort),\n    by = \"study_id\"\n  ) |>\n  filter(primary_for_cohort %in% c(\"Y\", \"partial\"))\n\ncat(\"step3 rows:\", nrow(step3), \"\\n\")\n\n# Pivot wide\ncmv_wide <- step3 |>\n  select(study_id, cohort_id, ptcy_used, events_n, denom_n,\n         first_author, pub_year, study_design) |>\n  pivot_wider(\n    id_cols    = c(study_id, cohort_id, first_author, pub_year, study_design),\n    names_from = ptcy_used,\n    values_from = c(events_n, denom_n),\n    values_fn  = first\n  ) |>\n  rename(ai = events_n_Y, ci = events_n_N,\n         n1i = denom_n_Y, n2i = denom_n_N) |>\n  filter(!is.na(ai), !is.na(ci), !is.na(n1i), !is.na(n2i)) |>\n  mutate(\n    bi = n1i - ai,\n    di = n2i - ci,\n    study_label = paste0(first_author, \" \", pub_year),\n    is_rct = study_design == \"RCT\"\n  )\n\ncat(sprintf(\"\\nPaired studies ready for metafor: %d  (RCTs: %d)\\n\",\n            nrow(cmv_wide), sum(cmv_wide$is_rct)))\n\ncmv_wide |>\n  arrange(desc(is_rct), study_label) |>\n  select(study_label, study_design, ai, n1i, ci, n2i) |>\n  print(n = 40)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01Q2v99bCXc6EStcdMN8TPG5"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# cohort_id split into .x (outcomes) and .y (studies); use .x\ncmv_wide <- step3 |>\n  selec...[truncated, total 1475 chars]","intent":"Build final CMV wide dataset and run metafor random-effects model","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Build final CMV wide dataset and run metafor random-effects model","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# cohort_id split into .x (outcomes) and .y (studies); Here's what we found — which is itself an important result for the analysis:

---

## Frequentist Sanity Check: CMV — Key Findings

### ⚠️ The RCT-subset is not usable for CMV meta-analysis

Out of 10 RCTs, only **Curtis 2025** has numeric CMV data for Comparison 1. The others fail for these reasons:

| RCT | Reason excluded from CMV meta-analysis |
|---|---|
| Bolaños-Meade 2023 (CTN_1703) | CMV extracted, but `event_count = NR` |
| Luznik 2022 (CTN_1301) | CMV extracted, but `event_count = NR` |
| Ueda Oshima 2025 | CMV extracted, but `event_count = NR` |
| Abedin 2025, Bolaños-Meade 2019, Fuchs 2021 | No CMV row extracted |
| Brissot 2024, Broers 2022, Xu 2025 | Comparison 2/3 only, not Comp 1 eligible |

This means CMV is **an observational-only outcome** in the Bayesian model — the RCT sensitivity analysis will need to use a different outcome (e.g. aGVHD or NRM).

### Full-corpus result: CMV any_reactivation D+100, Comparison 1

**k = 5 paired studies** (all retrospective). Result:

- **OR = 1.12** (95% CI: 0.78 – 1.62), p = 0.53
- τ² = 0.046, I² = 26% — **low heterogeneity**
- Q-test: p = 0.36 — no significant heterogeneity

**Interpretation:** No statistically significant difference in CMV any-reactivation at D+100 between PTCy and non-PTCy arms in observational data, but k=5 gives very low power. The wide CI is expected.

### Implication for the Bayesian analysis plan

The D+100 any-reactivation endpoint has **only 5 paired studies** — likely because most papers report `end_of_followup` or D+180 timepoints. We should probably use a **broader timepoint strategy** (pool D+100 + D+180, or use end-of-followup) for the Bayesian model.

I'll suggest some questions below:


<span class="suggestion">Check how many paired studies we get for CMV any_reactivation if we pool D+100 and D+180 timepoints (prioritizing D+100).</span>


<span class="suggestion">Run the frequentist check on a better-powered outcome — aGVHD grade II-IV at D+100, Comparison 1 — which has 753 outcome rows.</span>


<span class="suggestion">Use the end_of_followup timepoint for CMV to maximize k, then run metafor on that.</span>


<span class="suggestion">Show me the full CMV data landscape — all timepoints and subtypes — to decide which is the primary CMV endpoint for the Bayesian model.</span>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01SW6ANN6udAQNL4mKk1YK36"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"library(dplyr)\nlibrary(tidyr)\nlibrary(metafor)\n\n# ── Build CMV any_reactivation dataset: D...[truncated, total 2699 chars]","intent":"Build pooled CMV any_reactivation dataset with D+100 preferred, D+180 fallback, and pivot wide","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Build pooled CMV any_reactivation dataset with D+100 preferred, D+180 fallback, and pivot wide","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"library(dplyr)\nlibrary(tidyr)\nlibrary(metafor)\n\n# ── Build CMV any_reactivation dataset: D+100 preferred, D+180 fallback ──────\ncmv_pool <- outcomes |>\n  filter(\n    outcome_category      == \"CMV\",\n    outcome_subtype       == \"any_reactivation\",\n    timepoint             %in% c(\"D+100\", \"D+180\"),\n    is_primary_definition == \"Y\"\n  ) |>\n  mutate(\n    events_n = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(event_count)),\n    denom_n  = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(denominator_n)),\n    tp_rank  = ifelse(timepoint == \"D+100\", 1L, 2L)\n  ) |>\n  filter(!is.na(events_n), !is.na(denom_n)) |>\n  # Per arm: keep D+100 if available, else D+180\n  group_by(arm_id) |>\n  slice_min(tp_rank, n = 1, with_ties = FALSE) |>\n  ungroup() |>\n  left_join(\n    arms |> select(arm_id, study_id, comparison_1_eligible, ptcy_used, n_patients),\n    by = \"arm_id\", suffix = c(\"\", \"_arm\")\n  ) |>\n  filter(comparison_1_eligible == \"Y\") |>\n  left_join(\n    studies |> select(study_id, cohort_id, first_author, pub_year,\n                      study_design, primary_for_cohort),\n    by = \"study_id\"\n  ) |>\n  filter(primary_for_cohort %in% c(\"Y\", \"partial\"))\n\ncat(\"=== CMV any_reactivation: D+100 preferred, D+180 fallback ===\\n\")\ncat(sprintf(\"Total arms with data: %d\\n\", nrow(cmv_pool)))\ncmv_pool |> count(timepoint, ptcy_used) |> print()\n\n# ── Pivot wide ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\ncmv_pool_wide <- cmv_pool |>\n  select(study_id, cohort_id, ptcy_used, events_n, denom_n,\n         timepoint, first_author, pub_year, study_design) |>\n  # Where a study has BOTH timepoints (D+100 PTCy + D+180 ctrl, etc.) keep\n  # the arm-level best already selected above; now pivot\n  pivot_wider(\n    id_cols    = c(study_id, cohort_id, first_author, pub_year, study_design),\n    names_from = ptcy_used,\n    values_from = c(events_n, denom_n, timepoint),\n    values_fn  = first\n  ) |>\n  rename(ai = events_n_Y, ci = events_n_N,\n         n1i = denom_n_Y, n2i = denom_n_N,\n         tp_ptcy = timepoint_Y, tp_ctrl = timepoint_N) |>\n  filter(!is.na(ai), !is.na(ci), !is.na(n1i), !is.na(n2i)) |>\n  mutate(\n    bi = n1i - ai,\n    di = n2i - ci,\n    study_label = paste0(first_author, \" \", pub_year),\n    is_rct      = study_design == \"RCT\",\n    timepoint_used = case_when(\n      tp_ptcy == \"D+100\" & tp_ctrl == \"D+100\" ~ \"D+100 / D+100\",\n      tp_ptcy == \"D+180\" & tp_ctrl == \"D+180\" ~ \"D+180 / D+180\",\n      TRUE ~ paste0(tp_ptcy, \" / \", tp_ctrl)\n    )\n  )\n\ncat(sprintf(\"\\nPaired studies for metafor: %d  (RCTs: %d)\\n\",\n            nrow(cmv_pool_wide), sum(cmv_pool_wide$is_rct)))\n\ncmv_pool_wide |>\n  arrange(desc(is_rct), study_label) |>\n  select(study_label, study_design, timepoint_used, ai, n1i, ci, n2i) |>\n  print(n = 40)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01Co66iBjH7zMseAgMSp8iWR"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# cohort_id gets suffixed — use cohort_id.x (from outcomes)\ncmv_pool_wide <- cmv_pool |>\n ...[truncated, total 1278 chars]","intent":"Fix cohort_id suffix issue and print final paired dataset","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Fix cohort_id suffix issue and print final paired dataset","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# cohort_id gets suffixed — use cohort_id.x (from outcomes)\ncmv_pool_wide <- cmv_pool |>\n  select(study_id, cohort_id = cohort_id.x, ptcy_used, events_n, denom_n,\n         timepoint, first_author, pub_year, study_design) |>\n  pivot_wider(\n    id_cols    = c(study_id, cohort_id, first_author, pub_year, study_design),\n    names_from = ptcy_used,\n    values_from = c(events_n, denom_n, timepoint),\n    values_fn  = first\n  ) |>\n  rename(ai = events_n_Y, ci = events_n_N,\n         n1i = denom_n_Y, n2i = denom_n_N,\n         tp_ptcy = timepoint_Y, tp_ctrl = timepoint_N) |>\n  filter(!is.na(ai), !is.na(ci), !is.na(n1i), !is.na(n2i)) |>\n  mutate(\n    bi = n1i - ai,\n    di = n2i - ci,\n    study_label    = paste0(first_author, \" \", pub_year),\n    is_rct         = study_design == \"RCT\",\n    timepoint_used = paste0(coalesce(tp_ptcy,\"?\"), \" / \", coalesce(tp_ctrl,\"?\"))\n  )\n\ncat(sprintf(\"Paired studies: %d  (RCTs: %d)\\n\",\n            nrow(cmv_pool_wide), sum(cmv_pool_wide$is_rct)))\ncat(sprintf(\"Total patients: PTCy n=%d  |  Control n=%d\\n\",\n            sum(cmv_pool_wide$n1i, na.rm=TRUE),\n            sum(cmv_pool_wide$n2i, na.rm=TRUE)))\n\ncmv_pool_wide |>\n  arrange(desc(is_rct), pub_year) |>\n  select(study_label, study_design, timepoint_used, ai, n1i, ci, n2i) |>\n  print(n = 40)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01KroSaZeWVAp5St2g5fhFbR"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# ── Run metafor ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\ndat_pool ...[truncated, total 1113 chars]","intent":"Run metafor RE model and produce forest plot for pooled CMV any-reactivation dataset","language":"R","withPlot":true},"intent":"Run metafor RE model and produce forest plot for pooled CMV any-reactivation dataset","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# ── Run metafor ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\ndat_pool <- escalc(measure = \"OR\",\n                   ai = ai, bi = bi, ci = ci, di = di,\n                   data = cmv_pool_wide,\n                   slab = study_label)\n\nres_pool <- rma(yi, vi, data = dat_pool, method = \"REML\")\n\ncat(\"=== RE MODEL: CMV any_reactivation D+100|D+180, Comparison 1 (k=7) ===\\n\")\nprint(res_pool)\n\ncat(\"\\n--- OR scale (back-transformed) ---\\n\")\npredict(res_pool, transf = exp, digits = 3) |> print()\n\n# ── Forest plot ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nforest(res_pool,\n       transf   = exp,\n       refline  = 1,\n       xlab     = \"Odds Ratio (PTCy vs Control)\",\n       header   = TRUE,\n       mlab     = \"RE Model (REML)\",\n       col      = ifelse(dat_pool$study_design == \"RCT\", \"firebrick\", \"steelblue\"),\n       cex      = 0.9,\n       addfit   = TRUE,\n       at       = c(0.25, 0.5, 1, 2, 4, 8),\n       xlim     = c(-3, 5),\n       alim     = c(0.1, 8))\nmtext(\"CMV any-reactivation (D+100 preferred / D+180 fallback) — Comparison 1\",\n      side = 3, line = 1, cex = 0.85)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

Here's the summary:

---

## CMV any-reactivation: D+100 + D+180 pooled, Comparison 1

### Dataset
- Going from **k=5 (D+100 only)** → **k=7** by adding D+180 fallback — a modest gain (+2 studies: Tischer 2015 and Kerbauy 2021)
- All 7 studies are **retrospective cohorts** — no RCTs have numeric CMV data for Comparison 1
- Total N: PTCy = 403 patients, Control = 525 patients

### Model result
| Metric | Value |
|---|---|
| **OR (PTCy vs Control)** | **1.09** |
| 95% CI | 0.79 – 1.51 |
| Prediction interval | 0.67 – 1.80 |
| τ² | 0.037 (I² = 20%) |
| Q-test p | 0.29 |
| z p-value | 0.59 |

**Interpretation:** Still no statistically significant difference. The OR of 1.09 sits almost exactly on the null, with low heterogeneity (I² ≈ 20%). The wide prediction interval (0.67–1.80) reflects genuine uncertainty — consistent with the hypothesis that PTCy's immunosuppressive effect could push CMV either way. k=7 remains underpowered for a definitive frequentist conclusion, which is exactly where Bayesian borrowing adds value.

### What this tells us for the Bayesian plan
- **CMV is a low-k outcome** — even with timepoint pooling, we have only 7 paired observational studies
- The **prediction interval crossing 1 substantially** means the Bayesian model will need informative-but-weak priors, and the posterior will be largely data-driven
- The Bayesian model's main added value here will be the **GVHD-mediation adjustment** (controlling for PTCy's higher steroid exposure), not pooling power

I'll suggest some questions below:


<span class="suggestion">Now run the same frequentist check on aGVHD grade II-IV at D+100 — it's the most powered outcome (753 rows) and will tell us if the overall model framework is solid.</span>


<span class="suggestion">Check what the CMV landscape looks like if we also allow end_of_followup as a third-priority timepoint — how many more studies do we pick up?</span>


<span class="suggestion">Move on to setting up the Bayesian brms model for CMV — we have enough information to define the priors and model structure.</span>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01E1uzxH4wFwrQhFwLqL7CmB"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# ── Count PAIRED studies for the three candidate strategies ──────────────────\ncount_pair...[truncated, total 3042 chars]","intent":"Count paired studies across all candidate CMV endpoint strategies","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Count paired studies across all candidate CMV endpoint strategies","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# ── Count PAIRED studies for the three candidate strategies ──────────────────\ncount_paired <- function(subtypes, timepoints, priority_order, label) {\n  dat <- cmv_all |>\n    filter(outcome_subtype %in% subtypes,\n           timepoint %in% timepoints,\n           !is.na(events_n)) |>\n    mutate(tp_rank = match(timepoint, priority_order)) |>\n    group_by(arm_id) |>\n    slice_min(tp_rank, n = 1, with_ties = FALSE) |>\n    ungroup() |>\n    select(study_id, cohort_id = cohort_id.x, ptcy_used, events_n, denom_n,\n           timepoint, first_author, pub_year, study_design) |>\n    pivot_wider(\n      id_cols    = c(study_id, cohort_id, first_author, pub_year, study_design),\n      names_from = ptcy_used,\n      values_from = c(events_n, denom_n, timepoint),\n      values_fn  = first\n    )\n\n  # only keep fully paired rows\n  if (!\"events_n_Y\" %in% names(dat) || !\"events_n_N\" %in% names(dat)) {\n    cat(sprintf(\"%-55s  k=0  (no PTCy or no control arms)\\n\", label))\n    return(invisible(NULL))\n  }\n\n  paired <- dat |>\n    filter(!is.na(events_n_Y), !is.na(events_n_N),\n           !is.na(denom_n_Y),  !is.na(denom_n_N))\n\n  n_rct  <- sum(paired$study_design == \"RCT\", na.rm = TRUE)\n  n_ptcy <- sum(paired$denom_n_Y, na.rm = TRUE)\n  n_ctrl <- sum(paired$denom_n_N, na.rm = TRUE)\n  cat(sprintf(\"%-55s  k=%-3d  RCTs=%-2d  N_PTCy=%-5d  N_ctrl=%-5d\\n\",\n              label, nrow(paired), n_rct, as.integer(n_ptcy), as.integer(n_ctrl)))\n  invisible(paired)\n}\n\ncat(\"=== PAIRED STUDY COUNTS BY STRATEGY ===\\n\\n\")\n\n# Strategy A: any_reactivation, D+100 only\nstrat_A <- count_paired(\"any_reactivation\", \"D+100\", \"D+100\",\n                         \"A: any_react, D+100 only\")\n\n# Strategy B: any_reactivation, D+100 > D+180\nstrat_B <- count_paired(\"any_reactivation\", c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\"),\n                         c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\"),\n                         \"B: any_react, D+100 > D+180\")\n\n# Strategy C: any_reactivation, D+100 > D+180 > EoF\nstrat_C <- count_paired(\"any_reactivation\", c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\",\"end_of_followup\"),\n                         c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\",\"end_of_followup\"),\n                         \"C: any_react, D+100 > D+180 > EoF\")\n\n# Strategy D: any_reactivation + csCMV, D+100 > D+180 > EoF\nstrat_D <- count_paired(c(\"any_reactivation\",\"clinically_significant_csCMV\"),\n                         c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\",\"end_of_followup\"),\n                         c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\",\"end_of_followup\"),\n                         \"D: any_react+csCMV, D+100 > D+180 > EoF\")\n\n# Strategy E: EoF only (many papers report this as primary)\nstrat_E <- count_paired(\"any_reactivation\", \"end_of_followup\", \"end_of_followup\",\n                         \"E: any_react, EoF only\")\n\ncat(\"\\n--- Strategy C study list (the broadest any_reactivation set) ---\\n\")\nstrat_C |>\n  mutate(tp_ptcy = timepoint_Y, tp_ctrl = timepoint_N,\n         study_label = paste0(first_author, \" \", pub_year)) |>\n  arrange(desc(study_design == \"RCT\"), pub_year) |>\n  select(study_label, study_design, tp_ptcy, tp_ctrl,\n         events_n_Y, denom_n_Y, events_n_N, denom_n_N) |>\n  print(n = 40)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

return(invisible(NULL))\n  }\n\n  paired <- dat |>\n    filter(!is.na(events_n_Y), !is.na(events_n_N),\n           !is.na(denom_n_Y),  !is.na(denom_n_N))\n\n  n_rct  <- sum(paired$study_design == \"RCT\", na.rm = TRUE)\n  n_ptcy <- sum(paired$denom_n_Y, na.rm = TRUE)\n  n_ctrl <- sum(paired$denom_n_N, na.rm = TRUE)\n  cat(sprintf(\"%-55s  k=%-3d  RCTs=%-2d  N_PTCy=%-5d  N_ctrl=%-5d\\n\",\n              label, nrow(paired), n_rct, as.integer(n_ptcy), as.integer(n_ctrl)))\n  invisible(paired)\n}\n\ncat(\"=== PAIRED STUDY COUNTS BY STRATEGY ===\\n\\n\")\nstrat_A <- count_paired(\"any_reactivation\", \"D+100\", \"D+100\",\n                         \"A: any_react, D+100 only\")\nstrat_B <- count_paired(\"any_reactivation\", c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\"),\n                         c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\"),\n                         \"B: any_react, D+100 > D+180\")\nstrat_C <- count_paired(\"any_reactivation\", c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\",\"end_of_followup\"),\n                         c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\",\"end_of_followup\"),\n                         \"C: any_react, D+100 > D+180 > EoF\")\nstrat_D <- count_paired(c(\"any_reactivation\",\"clinically_significant_csCMV\"),\n                         c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\",\"end_of_followup\"),\n                         c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\",\"end_of_followup\"),\n                         \"D: any_react+csCMV, D+100 > D+180 > EoF\")\nstrat_E <- count_paired(\"any_reactivation\", \"end_of_followup\", \"end_of_followup\",\n                         \"E: any_react, EoF only\")\n\ncat(\"\\n--- Strategy C full study list ---\\n\")\nstrat_C |>\n  mutate(study_label = paste0(first_author, \" \", pub_year)) |>\n  arrange(desc(study_design == \"RCT\"), pub_year) |>\n  select(study_label, study_design,\n         tp_ptcy = timepoint_Y, tp_ctrl = timepoint_N,\n         ai = events_n_Y, n1i = denom_n_Y,\n         ci = events_n_N, n2i = denom_n_N) |>\n  print(n = 40)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01P4nVrreP1LoeVDgSt6B9oD"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"library(metafor)\n\n# ── Metafor on Strategy C (the primary recommendation) ────────────────...[truncated, total 1863 chars]","intent":"Run metafor on Strategy C and produce annotated forest plot","language":"R","withPlot":true},"intent":"Run metafor on Strategy C and produce annotated forest plot","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"library(metafor)\n\n# ── Metafor on Strategy C (the primary recommendation) ───────────────────────\ndat_C <- strat_C |>\n  mutate(\n    ai = events_n_Y, bi = denom_n_Y - events_n_Y,\n    ci = events_n_N, di = denom_n_N - events_n_N,\n    study_label = paste0(first_author, \" \", pub_year),\n    tp_label    = paste0(coalesce(timepoint_Y,\"?\"), \"/\", coalesce(timepoint_N,\"?\"))\n  ) |>\n  escalc(measure = \"OR\", ai = ai, bi = bi, ci = ci, di = di,\n         data = _, slab = study_label)\n\nres_C <- rma(yi, vi, data = dat_C, method = \"REML\")\n\ncat(\"=== RE MODEL: Strategy C — any_reactivation, D+100>D+180>EoF, k=18 ===\\n\")\nprint(res_C)\ncat(\"\\n--- OR (back-transformed) ---\\n\")\npredict(res_C, transf = exp, digits = 3) |> print()\n\n# ── Forest plot ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Colour by timepoint used\ntp_col <- ifelse(dat_C$timepoint_Y == \"D+100\", \"#2166ac\",\n          ifelse(dat_C$timepoint_Y == \"D+180\", \"#4dac26\", \"#d01c8b\"))\n\nforest(res_C,\n       transf   = exp,\n       refline  = 1,\n       xlab     = \"Odds Ratio (PTCy vs Control)\",\n       header   = c(\"Study\", \"OR [95% CI]\"),\n       mlab     = sprintf(\"RE Model (REML): OR=%.2f [%.2f–%.2f], I²=%.0f%%\",\n                          exp(res_C$b), exp(res_C$ci.lb), exp(res_C$ci.ub),\n                          res_C$I2),\n       col      = tp_col,\n       cex      = 0.82,\n       at       = c(0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1, 2, 4, 8),\n       xlim     = c(-4, 6),\n       alim     = c(0.1, 8),\n       ilab     = dat_C$tp_label,\n       ilab.xpos = 3.5,\n       ilab.pos  = 4)\n\nmtext(\"CMV any-reactivation (Strategy C: D+100 > D+180 > EoF)  —  Comparison 1\",\n      side = 3, line = 1, cex = 0.82, font = 2)\nmtext(\"Timepoint\", side = 3, line = -1.2, at = 3.5, cex = 0.72)\nlegend(\"topleft\", legend = c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\",\"End-of-FU\"),\n       col = c(\"#2166ac\",\"#4dac26\",\"#d01c8b\"), pch = 15, cex = 0.75, bty = \"n\")","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

The built-in metafor forest plot gets crowded at k=18 in this window — we'll do a proper ggplot2 version in the Bayesian step. Here's what matters from the numbers:

---

## Full CMV Data Landscape — Decision Summary

### Strategy comparison at a glance

| Strategy | Subtypes | Timepoints | k (paired) | RCTs | N PTCy | N Control | OR (REML) | I² |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A | any_react | D+100 only | 5 | 0 | 331 | 436 | — | — |
| B | any_react | D+100 > D+180 | 7 | 0 | 403 | 525 | 1.09 [0.79–1.51] | 20% |
| **C** | **any_react** | **D+100 > D+180 > EoF** | **18** | **0** | **973** | **1,929** | **1.22 [0.87–1.71]** | **69%** |
| D | any_react + csCMV | D+100 > D+180 > EoF | 20 | 1 | 1,062 | 2,056 | — | — |
| E | any_react | EoF only | 11 | 0 | 570 | 1,404 | — | — |

### Key finding: the heterogeneity jump

- **Strategies A–B** (early timepoints only): I² ≈ 20%, low heterogeneity
- **Strategy C** (adding EoF): I² jumps to **69%**, Q-test p < 0.0001

This makes biological sense — end-of-followup CMV accumulates over very different follow-up durations (1–5+ years), creating genuine between-study variation that isn't noise.

### Recommendation for the primary Bayesian model

**Strategy C is the right choice** for the Bayesian model, precisely *because* of the heterogeneity — that's what `brms` handles better than a frequentist pooled OR. The high I² means:
- The random-effects estimate (τ = 0.56) needs to be modelled, not averaged away
- The prediction interval (0.39–3.85) is the honest answer — individual study results vary widely
- The **GVHD-mediation covariate** may explain part of that heterogeneity

**Strategy D** (adding csCMV) picks up 2 more studies including Curtis 2025 (the only RCT) — worth including as a **sensitivity analysis** with a subtype indicator covariate.

I'll suggest some questions below:


<span class="suggestion">Set up the primary brms model for CMV any-reactivation Strategy C — define the model structure, priors, and run it.</span>


<span class="suggestion">First run the aGVHD frequentist sanity check (the most-powered outcome) before moving to brms, to validate the overall model framework.</span>


<span class="suggestion">Run a quick subgroup analysis in metafor on Strategy C — does the heterogeneity differ between D+100/D+180 studies vs EoF studies?</span>





## User

Let me check the schema for the GVHD covariate fields before building the dataset, then set up the full model:

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01UpQjYNTgNEa5yXkovVZMSd"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"library(dplyr)\n\n# ── Check availability of the GVHD mediation covariate ──────────────────...[truncated, total 2119 chars]","intent":"Check GVHD covariate availability in Strategy C arms","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Check GVHD covariate availability in Strategy C arms","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"library(dplyr)\n\n# ── Check availability of the GVHD mediation covariate ───────────────────────\n# We need arm-level aGVHD II-IV CI% and steroid exposure % for the covariate\n\ncat(\"=== GVHD covariate fields in arms ===\\n\")\nnames(arms)[grepl(\"steroid|gvhd|agvhd|aGVHD\", names(arms), ignore.case = TRUE)] |> print()\n\ncat(\"\\n=== aGVHD II-IV coverage in Strategy C arms ===\\n\")\n# Get arm_ids from strat_C\nstrat_C_armids <- cmv_all |>\n  filter(outcome_subtype == \"any_reactivation\",\n         timepoint %in% c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\",\"end_of_followup\"),\n         !is.na(suppressWarnings(as.numeric(event_count))),\n         is_primary_definition == \"Y\") |>\n  mutate(tp_rank = match(timepoint, c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\",\"end_of_followup\"))) |>\n  group_by(arm_id) |>\n  slice_min(tp_rank, n = 1, with_ties = FALSE) |>\n  ungroup() |>\n  # only arms in strat_C paired studies\n  filter(study_id %in% strat_C$study_id) |>\n  pull(arm_id) |> unique()\n\ncat(\"Unique arm_ids in Strategy C:\", length(strat_C_armids), \"\\n\\n\")\n\n# Pull aGVHD II-IV outcome for these arms\nagvhd_cov <- outcomes |>\n  filter(arm_id %in% strat_C_armids,\n         outcome_category == \"aGVHD\",\n         outcome_subtype  == \"grade_II_IV\",\n         timepoint        == \"D+100\",\n         is_primary_definition == \"Y\") |>\n  mutate(agvhd_ci_pct = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(cumulative_incidence_pct)),\n         agvhd_events = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(event_count)),\n         agvhd_denom  = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(denominator_n))) |>\n  select(arm_id, study_id, agvhd_ci_pct, agvhd_events, agvhd_denom)\n\ncat(\"aGVHD II-IV D+100 rows for Strategy C arms:\", nrow(agvhd_cov), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Arms with numeric aGVHD CI%:\", sum(!is.na(agvhd_cov$agvhd_ci_pct)), \"\\n\")\n\n# Pull systemic steroid exposure %\nsteroid_cov <- arms |>\n  filter(arm_id %in% strat_C_armids) |>\n  mutate(steroid_pct = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(systemic_steroid_for_gvhd_exposure_pct))) |>\n  select(arm_id, study_id, steroid_pct, n_patients, ptcy_used)\n\ncat(\"Arms with steroid_pct:\", sum(!is.na(steroid_cov$steroid_pct)), \"of\",\n    nrow(steroid_cov), \"\\n\")\n\nsteroid_cov |> count(ptcy_used, is.na(steroid_pct)) |> print()","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01UpQjYNTgNEa5yXkovVZMSd"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"content","value":[{"type":"text","text":"=== GVHD covariate fields in arms ===\n[1] \"systemic_steroid_for_gvhd_exposure_pct\"\n[2] \"st...[truncated, total 623 chars]"}]},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"=== GVHD covariate fields in arms ===\n[1] \"systemic_steroid_for_gvhd_exposure_pct\"\n[2] \"steroid_exposure_duration_days_median\" \n[3] \"second_line_gvhd_therapy_exposure_pct\" \n\n=== aGVHD II-IV coverage in Strategy C arms ===\nUnique arm_ids in Strategy C: 40 \n\naGVHD II-IV D+100 rows for Strategy C arms: 30 \nArms with numeric aGVHD CI%: 22 \nArms with steroid_pct: 30 of 40 \n# A tibble: 4 × 3\n  ptcy_used `is.na(steroid_pct)`     n\n  <chr>     <lgl>                <int>\n1 N         FALSE                   15\n2 N         TRUE                     5\n3 Y         FALSE                   15\n4 Y         TRUE                     5\n"},"elapsedMs":52}</script></tool-result></div>



<div><tool-call id="toolu_01GKzETb7BK5aUtW3SayCepD"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"library(dplyr)\nlibrary(tidyr)\n\n# ── Build the brms-ready analytic dataset ────────────────...[truncated, total 3749 chars]","intent":"Build arm-level brms analytic dataset with all covariates","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Build arm-level brms analytic dataset with all covariates","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"library(dplyr)\nlibrary(tidyr)\n\n# ── Build the brms-ready analytic dataset ─────────────────────────────────────\n# Unit of analysis: arm (not study pair)\n# Each arm contributes: events/n, PTCy indicator, arm-level covariates,\n#                       study_id (for random intercept), cohort_id (for clustering)\n\n# Step 1: CMV outcome rows for Strategy C arms\ncmv_brms_raw <- cmv_all |>\n  filter(outcome_subtype == \"any_reactivation\",\n         timepoint %in% c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\",\"end_of_followup\"),\n         !is.na(suppressWarnings(as.numeric(event_count))),\n         is_primary_definition == \"Y\") |>\n  mutate(\n    events_n = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(event_count)),\n    denom_n  = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(denominator_n)),\n    tp_rank  = match(timepoint, c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\",\"end_of_followup\"))\n  ) |>\n  group_by(arm_id) |>\n  slice_min(tp_rank, n = 1, with_ties = FALSE) |>\n  ungroup()\n\n# Step 2: join arm-level covariates\narm_covs <- arms |>\n  mutate(\n    arm_id      = as.integer(arm_id),\n    n_patients  = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(n_patients)),\n    steroid_pct = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(systemic_steroid_for_gvhd_exposure_pct)),\n    ptcy_binary = as.integer(ptcy_used == \"Y\"),\n    pub_year    = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(pub_year))\n  ) |>\n  select(arm_id, study_id, cohort_id, n_patients, ptcy_used, ptcy_binary,\n         steroid_pct, ptcy_dose_category,\n         conditioning_mac_pct, conditioning_ric_pct,\n         donor_haplo_pct, pub_year)\n\n# Step 3: join aGVHD II-IV CI% as alternative covariate\nagvhd_wide <- agvhd_cov |>\n  select(arm_id, agvhd_ci_pct)\n\ncmv_brms <- cmv_brms_raw |>\n  select(arm_id, study_id = study_id, cohort_id,\n         outcome_subtype, timepoint, events_n, denom_n) |>\n  left_join(arm_covs |> select(-study_id, -cohort_id), by = \"arm_id\") |>\n  left_join(agvhd_wide, by = \"arm_id\") |>\n  left_join(studies |> select(study_id, first_author, pub_year = pub_year,\n                               study_design, region, era_category),\n            by = \"study_id\") |>\n  # Only keep arms from the 18 paired studies\n  filter(study_id %in% strat_C$study_id) |>\n  mutate(\n    # Centre the GVHD covariate at grand mean for interpretability\n    steroid_pct_c  = steroid_pct  - mean(steroid_pct, na.rm = TRUE),\n    agvhd_ci_pct_c = agvhd_ci_pct - mean(agvhd_ci_pct, na.rm = TRUE),\n    # Era: pre-2018 vs 2018+\n    era_modern = as.integer(pub_year.x >= 2018),\n    # Timepoint flag for sensitivity\n    tp_d100_d180 = as.integer(timepoint %in% c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\"))\n  )\n\ncat(\"=== BRMS ANALYTIC DATASET ===\\n\")\ncat(sprintf(\"Rows (arms): %d  |  Studies: %d  |  Cohorts: %d\\n\",\n            nrow(cmv_brms), n_distinct(cmv_brms$study_id),\n            n_distinct(cmv_brms$cohort_id)))\ncat(sprintf(\"PTCy arms: %d  |  Control arms: %d\\n\",\n            sum(cmv_brms$ptcy_binary == 1), sum(cmv_brms$ptcy_binary == 0)))\ncat(sprintf(\"Total events: %d / %d patients\\n\",\n            sum(cmv_brms$events_n, na.rm=TRUE),\n            sum(cmv_brms$denom_n, na.rm=TRUE)))\n\ncat(\"\\n--- Covariate availability ---\\n\")\ncat(sprintf(\"steroid_pct:  %d / %d arms non-missing (%.0f%%)\\n\",\n            sum(!is.na(cmv_brms$steroid_pct)), nrow(cmv_brms),\n            100*mean(!is.na(cmv_brms$steroid_pct))))\ncat(sprintf(\"agvhd_ci_pct: %d / %d arms non-missing (%.0f%%)\\n\",\n            sum(!is.na(cmv_brms$agvhd_ci_pct)), nrow(cmv_brms),\n            100*mean(!is.na(cmv_brms$agvhd_ci_pct))))\n\ncat(\"\\n--- Timepoint distribution ---\\n\")\ncmv_brms |> count(timepoint, ptcy_used) |> print()\n\ncat(\"\\n--- Study design ---\\n\")\ncmv_brms |> count(study_design) |> print()\n\ncat(\"\\n--- Key columns preview ---\\n\")\ncmv_brms |>\n  select(study_id, first_author, ptcy_used, timepoint,\n         events_n, denom_n, steroid_pct, agvhd_ci_pct) |>\n  arrange(study_id) |>\n  print(n = 40)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

mean(steroid_pct,  na.rm = TRUE),\n    agvhd_ci_pct_c = agvhd_ci_pct - mean(agvhd_ci_pct, na.rm = TRUE),\n    era_modern     = as.integer(pub_year >= 2018),\n    tp_early       = as.integer(timepoint %in% c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\"))\n  )\n\ncat(\"=== BRMS ANALYTIC DATASET ===\\n\")\ncat(sprintf(\"Rows (arms): %d  |  Studies: %d  |  Cohorts: %d\\n\",\n            nrow(cmv_brms), n_distinct(cmv_brms$study_id),\n            n_distinct(cmv_brms$cohort_id)))\ncat(sprintf(\"PTCy arms: %d  |  Control arms: %d\\n\",\n            sum(cmv_brms$ptcy_binary==1), sum(cmv_brms$ptcy_binary==0)))\ncat(sprintf(\"Total events: %d / %d patients\\n\",\n            sum(cmv_brms$events_n,na.rm=TRUE), sum(cmv_brms$denom_n,na.rm=TRUE)))\ncat(sprintf(\"\\nsteroid_pct:  %d / %d arms (%.0f%%)\\n\",\n            sum(!is.na(cmv_brms$steroid_pct)), nrow(cmv_brms),\n            100*mean(!is.na(cmv_brms$steroid_pct))))\ncat(sprintf(\"agvhd_ci_pct: %d / %d arms (%.0f%%)\\n\",\n            sum(!is.na(cmv_brms$agvhd_ci_pct)), nrow(cmv_brms),\n            100*mean(!is.na(cmv_brms$agvhd_ci_pct))))\n\ncat(\"\\n--- Timepoint × PTCy arm ---\\n\")\ncmv_brms |> count(timepoint, ptcy_used) |> print()\n\ncat(\"\\n--- Full dataset preview ---\\n\")\ncmv_brms |>\n  select(study_id, first_author, ptcy_used, timepoint,\n         events_n, denom_n, steroid_pct, agvhd_ci_pct) |>\n  arrange(study_id) |>\n  print(n = 40)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_018b7enknb95p3Q8KYd9bsd8"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# cohort_id exists in cmv_brms_raw — the problem is the subsequent join\n# with arm_covs al...[truncated, total 2103 chars]","intent":"Rebuild cmv_brms cleanly, avoiding cohort_id join collision","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Rebuild cmv_brms cleanly, avoiding cohort_id join collision","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# cohort_id exists in cmv_brms_raw — the problem is the subsequent join\n# with arm_covs also has cohort_id; use suffix to avoid collision\n\ncmv_brms <- cmv_brms_raw |>\n  select(arm_id, study_id, cohort_id,\n         outcome_subtype, timepoint, events_n, denom_n) |>\n  left_join(\n    arm_covs |> select(-study_id, -cohort_id),   # drop duplicates before join\n    by = \"arm_id\"\n  ) |>\n  left_join(agvhd_wide, by = \"arm_id\") |>\n  left_join(\n    studies |>\n      mutate(study_id = as.integer(study_id),\n             pub_year  = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(pub_year))) |>\n      select(study_id, first_author, pub_year, study_design, region, era_category),\n    by = \"study_id\"\n  ) |>\n  filter(study_id %in% strat_C$study_id) |>\n  mutate(\n    steroid_pct_c  = steroid_pct  - mean(steroid_pct,  na.rm = TRUE),\n    agvhd_ci_pct_c = agvhd_ci_pct - mean(agvhd_ci_pct, na.rm = TRUE),\n    era_modern     = as.integer(pub_year >= 2018),\n    tp_early       = as.integer(timepoint %in% c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\"))\n  )\n\ncat(\"=== BRMS ANALYTIC DATASET ===\\n\")\ncat(sprintf(\"Rows (arms): %d  |  Studies: %d  |  Cohorts: %d\\n\",\n            nrow(cmv_brms), n_distinct(cmv_brms$study_id),\n            n_distinct(cmv_brms$cohort_id)))\ncat(sprintf(\"PTCy arms: %d  |  Control arms: %d\\n\",\n            sum(cmv_brms$ptcy_binary==1), sum(cmv_brms$ptcy_binary==0)))\ncat(sprintf(\"Total events: %d / %d patients\\n\",\n            sum(cmv_brms$events_n,na.rm=TRUE), sum(cmv_brms$denom_n,na.rm=TRUE)))\n\ncat(sprintf(\"\\nsteroid_pct available:  %d / %d arms (%.0f%%)\\n\",\n            sum(!is.na(cmv_brms$steroid_pct)), nrow(cmv_brms),\n            100*mean(!is.na(cmv_brms$steroid_pct))))\ncat(sprintf(\"agvhd_ci_pct available: %d / %d arms (%.0f%%)\\n\",\n            sum(!is.na(cmv_brms$agvhd_ci_pct)), nrow(cmv_brms),\n            100*mean(!is.na(cmv_brms$agvhd_ci_pct))))\n\ncat(\"\\n--- Timepoint × PTCy ---\\n\")\ncmv_brms |> count(timepoint, ptcy_used) |> print()\n\ncat(\"\\n--- Full preview ---\\n\")\ncmv_brms |>\n  select(study_id, first_author, ptcy_used, timepoint,\n         events_n, denom_n, steroid_pct, agvhd_ci_pct) |>\n  arrange(study_id) |>\n  print(n = 40)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_014sDD9NX6EqCWkaiztZrrWM"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# Rebuild arm_covs and cmv_brms cleanly in one block\narm_covs <- arms |>\n  mutate(\n    arm...[truncated, total 2371 chars]","intent":"Rebuild arm_covs and cmv_brms in one clean block","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Rebuild arm_covs and cmv_brms in one clean block","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# Rebuild arm_covs and cmv_brms cleanly in one block\narm_covs <- arms |>\n  mutate(\n    arm_id      = as.integer(arm_id),\n    n_patients  = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(n_patients)),\n    steroid_pct = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(systemic_steroid_for_gvhd_exposure_pct)),\n    ptcy_binary = as.integer(ptcy_used == \"Y\")\n  ) |>\n  select(arm_id, n_patients, ptcy_used, ptcy_binary, steroid_pct,\n         ptcy_dose_category, conditioning_mac_pct, conditioning_ric_pct,\n         donor_haplo_pct)\n\ncmv_brms <- cmv_brms_raw |>\n  select(arm_id, study_id, cohort_id,\n         outcome_subtype, timepoint, events_n, denom_n) |>\n  left_join(arm_covs, by = \"arm_id\") |>\n  left_join(agvhd_wide, by = \"arm_id\") |>\n  left_join(\n    studies |>\n      mutate(study_id = as.integer(study_id),\n             pub_year  = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(pub_year))) |>\n      select(study_id, first_author, pub_year, study_design, region, era_category),\n    by = \"study_id\"\n  ) |>\n  filter(study_id %in% strat_C$study_id) |>\n  mutate(\n    steroid_pct_c  = steroid_pct  - mean(steroid_pct,  na.rm = TRUE),\n    agvhd_ci_pct_c = agvhd_ci_pct - mean(agvhd_ci_pct, na.rm = TRUE),\n    era_modern     = as.integer(pub_year >= 2018),\n    tp_early       = as.integer(timepoint %in% c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\"))\n  )\n\ncat(\"=== BRMS ANALYTIC DATASET ===\\n\")\ncat(sprintf(\"Rows (arms): %d  |  Studies: %d  |  Cohorts: %d\\n\",\n            nrow(cmv_brms), n_distinct(cmv_brms$study_id),\n            n_distinct(cmv_brms$cohort_id)))\ncat(sprintf(\"PTCy arms: %d  |  Control arms: %d\\n\",\n            sum(cmv_brms$ptcy_binary==1), sum(cmv_brms$ptcy_binary==0)))\ncat(sprintf(\"Total events: %d / %d patients\\n\",\n            sum(cmv_brms$events_n,na.rm=TRUE), sum(cmv_brms$denom_n,na.rm=TRUE)))\ncat(sprintf(\"\\nsteroid_pct available:  %d/%d arms (%.0f%%)\\n\",\n            sum(!is.na(cmv_brms$steroid_pct)), nrow(cmv_brms),\n            100*mean(!is.na(cmv_brms$steroid_pct))))\ncat(sprintf(\"agvhd_ci_pct available: %d/%d arms (%.0f%%)\\n\",\n            sum(!is.na(cmv_brms$agvhd_ci_pct)), nrow(cmv_brms),\n            100*mean(!is.na(cmv_brms$agvhd_ci_pct))))\n\ncat(\"\\n--- Timepoint × PTCy ---\\n\")\ncmv_brms |> count(timepoint, ptcy_used) |> print()\n\ncat(\"\\n--- Full preview ---\\n\")\ncmv_brms |>\n  select(study_id, first_author, ptcy_used, timepoint,\n         events_n, denom_n, steroid_pct, agvhd_ci_pct) |>\n  arrange(study_id) |>\n  print(n = 40)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01WgDuAy6DHdRkBpLPtQmxj4"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"library(dplyr)\nlibrary(tidyr)\n\n# ── 1. Identify Strategy-C study IDs ─────────────────────...[truncated, total 3624 chars]","intent":"Clean full rebuild of brms analytic dataset from scratch in one block","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Clean full rebuild of brms analytic dataset from scratch in one block","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"library(dplyr)\nlibrary(tidyr)\n\n# ── 1. Identify Strategy-C study IDs ─────────────────────────────────────────\nstrat_C_study_ids <- strat_C$study_id\n\n# ── 2. Get all CMV any_reactivation rows (D+100 > D+180 > EoF priority) ──────\ncmv_brms_raw <- cmv_all |>\n  filter(outcome_subtype == \"any_reactivation\",\n         timepoint %in% c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\",\"end_of_followup\"),\n         !is.na(suppressWarnings(as.numeric(event_count))),\n         is_primary_definition == \"Y\",\n         study_id %in% strat_C_study_ids) |>\n  mutate(\n    events_n = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(event_count)),\n    denom_n  = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(denominator_n)),\n    tp_rank  = match(timepoint, c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\",\"end_of_followup\"))\n  ) |>\n  group_by(arm_id) |>\n  slice_min(tp_rank, n = 1, with_ties = FALSE) |>\n  ungroup()\n\n# ── 3. Arm-level covariates ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\narm_covs <- arms |>\n  mutate(\n    arm_id      = as.integer(arm_id),\n    n_patients  = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(n_patients)),\n    steroid_pct = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(systemic_steroid_for_gvhd_exposure_pct)),\n    ptcy_binary = as.integer(ptcy_used == \"Y\")\n  ) |>\n  select(arm_id, n_patients, ptcy_used, ptcy_binary, steroid_pct, ptcy_dose_category)\n\n# ── 4. aGVHD II-IV CI% covariate ─────────────────────────────────────────────\nagvhd_wide <- outcomes |>\n  filter(outcome_category    == \"aGVHD\",\n         outcome_subtype     == \"grade_II_IV\",\n         timepoint           == \"D+100\",\n         is_primary_definition == \"Y\",\n         study_id %in% strat_C_study_ids) |>\n  mutate(agvhd_ci_pct = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(cumulative_incidence_pct))) |>\n  select(arm_id, agvhd_ci_pct)\n\n# ── 5. Assemble ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nstudy_meta <- studies |>\n  mutate(study_id = as.integer(study_id),\n         pub_year = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(pub_year))) |>\n  select(study_id, first_author, pub_year, study_design, region, era_category)\n\ncmv_brms <- cmv_brms_raw |>\n  select(arm_id, study_id, cohort_id,\n         outcome_subtype, timepoint, events_n, denom_n) |>\n  left_join(arm_covs,    by = \"arm_id\") |>\n  left_join(agvhd_wide,  by = \"arm_id\") |>\n  left_join(study_meta,  by = \"study_id\") |>\n  mutate(\n    steroid_pct_c  = steroid_pct  - mean(steroid_pct,  na.rm = TRUE),\n    agvhd_ci_pct_c = agvhd_ci_pct - mean(agvhd_ci_pct, na.rm = TRUE),\n    era_modern     = as.integer(pub_year >= 2018),\n    tp_early       = as.integer(timepoint %in% c(\"D+100\",\"D+180\"))\n  )\n\ncat(\"=== BRMS ANALYTIC DATASET — CMV any_reactivation Strategy C ===\\n\")\ncat(sprintf(\"Arms: %d  |  Studies: %d  |  Cohorts: %d\\n\",\n            nrow(cmv_brms), n_distinct(cmv_brms$study_id), n_distinct(cmv_brms$cohort_id)))\ncat(sprintf(\"PTCy: %d  |  Control: %d\\n\",\n            sum(cmv_brms$ptcy_binary==1), sum(cmv_brms$ptcy_binary==0)))\ncat(sprintf(\"Events: %d / %d  (overall rate %.1f%%)\\n\",\n            sum(cmv_brms$events_n), sum(cmv_brms$denom_n),\n            100*sum(cmv_brms$events_n)/sum(cmv_brms$denom_n)))\ncat(sprintf(\"\\nsteroid_pct:  %d/%d (%.0f%%)\\n\",\n            sum(!is.na(cmv_brms$steroid_pct)), nrow(cmv_brms),\n            100*mean(!is.na(cmv_brms$steroid_pct))))\ncat(sprintf(\"agvhd_ci_pct: %d/%d (%.0f%%)\\n\",\n            sum(!is.na(cmv_brms$agvhd_ci_pct)), nrow(cmv_brms),\n            100*mean(!is.na(cmv_brms$agvhd_ci_pct))))\ncat(\"\\n--- Timepoint × arm type ---\\n\")\ncmv_brms |> count(timepoint, ptcy_used) |> print()\n\ncat(\"\\n--- Full dataset ---\\n\")\ncmv_brms |>\n  select(study_id, first_author, ptcy_used, timepoint,\n         events_n, denom_n, steroid_pct, agvhd_ci_pct) |>\n  arrange(study_id) |>\n  print(n = 40)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

Good — but I can see a data structure issue that needs handling before we run brms: **studies 5 (Khimani) and 183 (Jamy) have 3–4 rows each** (multiple arms per study). Let me handle that, then define and run the model:

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01RDb9xT7daDnLzVY5gL9MG5"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"cat(\"=== MULTI-ARM STUDIES ===\\n\")\ncmv_brms |> count(study_id, first_author) |> filter(n >...[truncated, total 2171 chars]","intent":"Identify and resolve multi-arm studies, produce clean one-row-per-arm dataset","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Identify and resolve multi-arm studies, produce clean one-row-per-arm dataset","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"cat(\"=== MULTI-ARM STUDIES ===\\n\")\ncmv_brms |> count(study_id, first_author) |> filter(n > 2) |>\n  left_join(cmv_brms |> select(study_id, ptcy_used, arm_id, denom_n,\n                                events_n, timepoint),\n            by = \"study_id\") |>\n  select(study_id, first_author, arm_id, ptcy_used, events_n, denom_n, timepoint) |>\n  arrange(study_id) |> print()\n\n# Study 5 (Khimani): has 2 PTCy and 2 control arms — likely haplo vs non-haplo\n# Study 183 (Jamy): has 2 PTCy arms (matched + unmatched)\n# Approach: for multi-PTCy or multi-control, sum events/denoms if same study_id\n# (i.e., treat as one pooled PTCy and one pooled control arm per study)\n\ncmv_brms_clean <- cmv_brms |>\n  group_by(study_id, cohort_id, first_author, pub_year, study_design,\n           region, era_category, ptcy_used, ptcy_binary,\n           timepoint, tp_early, era_modern) |>\n  summarise(\n    events_n       = sum(events_n, na.rm = TRUE),\n    denom_n        = sum(denom_n,  na.rm = TRUE),\n    steroid_pct    = mean(steroid_pct, na.rm = TRUE),\n    agvhd_ci_pct   = mean(agvhd_ci_pct, na.rm = TRUE),\n    steroid_pct_c  = mean(steroid_pct_c, na.rm = TRUE),\n    agvhd_ci_pct_c = mean(agvhd_ci_pct_c, na.rm = TRUE),\n    n_arms_merged  = n(),\n    .groups = \"drop\"\n  ) |>\n  # Re-centre after aggregation\n  mutate(\n    steroid_pct_c  = steroid_pct  - mean(steroid_pct,  na.rm = TRUE),\n    agvhd_ci_pct_c = agvhd_ci_pct - mean(agvhd_ci_pct, na.rm = TRUE)\n  )\n\ncat(\"\\n=== CLEAN DATASET (one PTCy row + one control row per study) ===\\n\")\ncat(sprintf(\"Rows: %d  |  Studies: %d\\n\", nrow(cmv_brms_clean), n_distinct(cmv_brms_clean$study_id)))\ncat(sprintf(\"PTCy: %d  |  Control: %d\\n\",\n            sum(cmv_brms_clean$ptcy_binary==1), sum(cmv_brms_clean$ptcy_binary==0)))\ncat(sprintf(\"Events: %d / %d  (%.1f%%)\\n\",\n            sum(cmv_brms_clean$events_n), sum(cmv_brms_clean$denom_n),\n            100*sum(cmv_brms_clean$events_n)/sum(cmv_brms_clean$denom_n)))\ncat(sprintf(\"steroid_pct non-NA: %d/%d\\n\",\n            sum(!is.na(cmv_brms_clean$steroid_pct)), nrow(cmv_brms_clean)))\ncat(sprintf(\"agvhd_ci_pct non-NA: %d/%d\\n\",\n            sum(!is.na(cmv_brms_clean$agvhd_ci_pct)), nrow(cmv_brms_clean)))","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01RDb9xT7daDnLzVY5gL9MG5"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"content","value":[{"type":"text","text":"=== MULTI-ARM STUDIES ===\n# A tibble: 10 × 7\n   study_id first_author arm_id ptcy_used eve...[truncated, total 988 chars]"}]},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"=== MULTI-ARM STUDIES ===\n# A tibble: 10 × 7\n   study_id first_author arm_id ptcy_used events_n denom_n\n      <dbl> <chr>         <dbl> <chr>        <dbl>   <dbl>\n 1        5 Khimani F         6 Y               38      75\n 2        5 Khimani F         7 Y               10      38\n 3        5 Khimani F         8 N               23      89\n 4        5 Khimani F         9 N               50     381\n 5      167 Haebe S         174 Y                3       8\n 6      167 Haebe S         175 N                2      11\n 7      167 Haebe S         176 N                0       8\n 8      183 Jamy O          190 N               27      94\n 9      183 Jamy O          191 Y               40     103\n10      183 Jamy O          192 Y               17      28\n# ℹ 1 more variable: timepoint <chr>\n\n=== CLEAN DATASET (one PTCy row + one control row per study) ===\nRows: 36  |  Studies: 18\nPTCy: 18  |  Control: 18\nEvents: 1493 / 3357  (44.5%)\nsteroid_pct non-NA: 28/36\nagvhd_ci_pct non-NA: 20/36\n"},"elapsedMs":28}</script></tool-result></div>



<div><tool-call id="toolu_01Jrax3bvR8hHrFR3BgU3Zyo"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"library(brms)\n\n# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════...[truncated, total 2422 chars]","intent":"Define priors and run brms M1 direct-effect model for CMV any-reactivation","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Define priors and run brms M1 direct-effect model for CMV any-reactivation","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"library(brms)\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n# MODEL M1: Direct-effect model (no GVHD covariate)\n#\n# Likelihood:  events_n | trials(denom_n) ~ Binomial\n# Link:        logit\n# Fixed:       ptcy_binary  (log-OR of PTCy vs control)\n#              tp_early     (sensitivity: D+100/D+180 vs EoF timepoint)\n# Random:      (1 | study_id)  — between-study heterogeneity (tau)\n#\n# Priors (weakly informative, per Bayesian MA convention):\n#   beta_ptcy   ~ Normal(0, 0.82)   => SD on log-OR scale ≈ 0.82\n#                                      = OR of ~2.3 at 1 SD — plausible clinical range\n#   beta_tp     ~ Normal(0, 0.5)    => accounts for timepoint mixing; tight\n#   Intercept   ~ Normal(0, 1.5)    => baseline event rate: logit^-1(-1.5 to 1.5) ≈ 18-82%\n#   tau (sd_1)  ~ HalfNormal(0, 0.5) => prior on between-study SD; conservative\n#                                        based on our metafor I² estimate\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\npriors_m1 <- c(\n  prior(normal(0, 0.82), class = b,       coef = ptcy_binary),\n  prior(normal(0, 0.50), class = b,       coef = tp_early),\n  prior(normal(0, 1.50), class = Intercept),\n  prior(normal(0, 0.50), class = sd,      group = study_id)\n)\n\ncat(\"=== Prior predictive check: implied event-rate range ===\\n\")\n# What does Normal(0, 1.5) intercept imply?\nset.seed(42)\nalpha_sims <- rnorm(10000, 0, 1.5)\ncat(sprintf(\"Baseline rate prior: median=%.1f%%, 5th=%.1f%%, 95th=%.1f%%\\n\",\n            100*median(plogis(alpha_sims)),\n            100*quantile(plogis(alpha_sims), 0.05),\n            100*quantile(plogis(alpha_sims), 0.95)))\n\nbeta_sims <- rnorm(10000, 0, 0.82)\ncat(sprintf(\"PTCy OR prior: median=%.2f, 5th=%.2f, 95th=%.2f\\n\",\n            median(exp(beta_sims)),\n            quantile(exp(beta_sims), 0.05),\n            quantile(exp(beta_sims), 0.95)))\n\ncat(\"\\n=== Running M1 (direct-effect, no GVHD covariate) ===\\n\")\ncat(\"Dataset: k=18 studies, 36 arms, n=3357 patients\\n\\n\")\n\nm1 <- brm(\n  formula  = events_n | trials(denom_n) ~ ptcy_binary + tp_early + (1 | study_id),\n  data     = cmv_brms_clean,\n  family   = binomial(link = \"logit\"),\n  prior    = priors_m1,\n  chains   = 4,\n  iter     = 4000,\n  warmup   = 1000,\n  cores    = 4,\n  seed     = 2026,\n  backend  = \"cmdstanr\",\n  silent   = 2,\n  refresh  = 0\n)\n\ncat(\"\\n=== M1 POSTERIOR SUMMARY ===\\n\")\nprint(summary(m1), digits = 3)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01BJJTyeDJAmHtozc5YSMSS9"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# Use rstan backend instead of cmdstanr\nm1 <- brm(\n  formula  = events_n | trials(denom_n)...[truncated, total 441 chars]","intent":"Re-run M1 with rstan backend","language":"R","withPlot":false},"intent":"Re-run M1 with rstan backend","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# Use rstan backend instead of cmdstanr\nm1 <- brm(\n  formula  = events_n | trials(denom_n) ~ ptcy_binary + tp_early + (1 | study_id),\n  data     = cmv_brms_clean,\n  family   = binomial(link = \"logit\"),\n  prior    = priors_m1,\n  chains   = 4,\n  iter     = 4000,\n  warmup   = 1000,\n  cores    = 4,\n  seed     = 2026,\n  backend  = \"rstan\",\n  silent   = 2,\n  refresh  = 0\n)\n\ncat(\"\\n=== M1 POSTERIOR SUMMARY ===\\n\")\nprint(summary(m1), digits = 3)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>
